{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-5-20251101","id":"msg_01RZCqzcqnuUp9DjUVZUvrUA","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01YNBp3S9pK8FfSppHHWrSyM","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/logs/agent/solution.md","content":"# Architectural Proposal: Real-Time 'Hardcore Mode' Synchronization\n\n**Document Status**: Proposal\n**Date**: 2026-02-02\n**Author**: Lead Architect\n**ADR Reference**: ADR-003-Offline-Sync-Strategy.md\n\n---\n\n## Executive Summary\n\nThis document presents a high-level architectural plan for introducing a new 'Hardcore Mode' feature that requires real-time synchronization of player actions with the server. The proposal maintains full backward compatibility with the existing offline-first architecture while introducing a clean abstraction layer that enables conditional synchronization behavior based on player game mode.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Analysis of Conflict\n\n### 1.1 Current Architecture (ADR-003)\n\nThe existing TycoonVerse architecture is explicitly designed around an **offline-first** strategy as documented in `docs/design/ADR-003-Offline-Sync-Strategy.md`. The key characteristics are:\n\n1. **Client-Side Event Sourcing**: All player actions are captured locally in an append-only SQLite table (`GameEventLog`) with metadata including `PlayerId`, `Sequence`, `VectorClock`, and `Checksum` (ADR-003:37-40).\n\n2. **Deferred Synchronization**: The `OfflineSyncManager` coordinates periodic background sync sessions, pushing local events to the server only when connectivity is available. The sync pump runs on a 15-second interval (ADR-003:189).\n\n3. **Queued Actions**: When offline, actions are queued locally via the `IOfflineRequestStore` interface and `IPlayerActionRepository`. The `SyncPlayerActionsCommand` handler (`src/TycoonVerse.Application/Features/Sync/SyncPlayerActionsCommand.cs:56-149`) processes pending actions in batches and sends them to the server.\n\n4. **Eventually Consistent Model**: The system is designed for eventual consistency with CRDT-inspired merge algorithms for conflict resolution (ADR-003:34-35).\n\n### 1.2 Hardcore Mode Requirements Conflict\n\nThe 'Hardcore Mode' requirement directly conflicts with this architecture because:\n\n| Aspect | Current (Offline-First) | Hardcore Mode (Real-Time) |\n|--------|------------------------|---------------------------|\n| **Sync Timing** | Periodic (every 15s) or on reconnect | Immediate, before action confirmation |\n| **Failure Handling** | Queue and retry later | Block action until server confirms |\n| **Network Dependency** | Optional (graceful degradation) | Mandatory (action rejected if offline) |\n| **Consistency Model** | Eventually consistent | Strongly consistent |\n| **Source of Truth** | Local until sync | Server-side always |\n\n### 1.3 Key Source Files Impacted\n\nThe following existing components embody the offline-first design and would require modification:\n\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Application/Features/Sync/SyncPlayerActionsCommand.cs` - Current batch sync orchestrator\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Infrastructure/Networking/ApiClient.cs:97-101` - Queues requests when offline\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Application/Common/EventBus.cs:159-163` - Always enqueues to offline store\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Infrastructure/Persistence/UnitOfWork.cs` - Current save behavior\n\n---\n\n## 2. Proposed Architectural Pattern\n\n### 2.1 Primary Pattern: Strategy Pattern\n\nThe **Strategy Pattern** is the most appropriate solution for this requirement. This behavioral design pattern allows us to:\n\n1. **Define a family of algorithms** (synchronization strategies)\n2. **Encapsulate each one** in its own class\n3. **Make them interchangeable** at runtime based on player context\n\n### 2.2 Why Strategy Pattern is Superior to Naive Approaches\n\n#### Naive Approach (Anti-Pattern)\n```csharp\n// ANTI-PATTERN: Do not implement this way\npublic async Task<CreateCompanyResult> Handle(CreateCompanyCommand request, CancellationToken ct)\n{\n    // Business logic...\n\n    if (player.IsHardcoreMode)\n    {\n        await _apiClient.SendImmediateAsync(action, ct);\n        if (!response.IsSuccess)\n            throw new SyncFailedException(\"Server rejected action\");\n    }\n    else\n    {\n        await _actionRepository.EnqueueAsync(action, ct);\n    }\n}\n```\n\n**Problems with this approach:**\n\n1. **Violates Open/Closed Principle (OCP)**: Every command handler must be modified when adding new sync modes (e.g., \"Tournament Mode\", \"Tutorial Mode\").\n\n2. **Code Duplication**: The `if (isHardcore)` check would be repeated in every command handler (`CreateCompanyCommand`, `FileIPOCommand`, `ExecuteTradeCommand`, etc.).\n\n3. **Tight Coupling**: Command handlers become coupled to both sync implementations, making unit testing complex.\n\n4. **Maintenance Nightmare**: Adding a third sync mode requires touching every handler.\n\n5. **Cognitive Load**: Developers must remember to add the check in new handlers.\n\n#### Strategy Pattern Approach (Recommended)\n\n```csharp\n// Command handler remains clean and single-responsibility\npublic async Task<CreateCompanyResult> Handle(CreateCompanyCommand request, CancellationToken ct)\n{\n    // Business logic...\n\n    var action = PlayerAction.Create(/* ... */);\n    await _syncStrategy.SyncAsync(action, ct); // Strategy handles the how\n}\n```\n\n**Benefits:**\n\n1. **Open/Closed Principle**: New sync modes require only new strategy classes, no modifications to handlers.\n\n2. **Single Responsibility**: Each strategy class has one job - implementing a specific sync behavior.\n\n3. **Testability**: Mock `IActionSyncStrategy` in unit tests without complex setup.\n\n4. **Runtime Flexibility**: Switch strategies based on player state, network conditions, or A/B tests.\n\n5. **Clean Separation**: Business logic (what to do) is separated from infrastructure concerns (how to sync).\n\n### 2.3 Supporting Patterns\n\n| Pattern | Purpose | Location |\n|---------|---------|----------|\n| **Strategy** | Encapsulate sync algorithms | `IActionSyncStrategy` interface + implementations |\n| **Factory** | Create correct strategy based on player state | `IActionSyncStrategyFactory` |\n| **Decorator** (optional) | Add cross-cutting concerns like logging, metrics | `MetricsDecoratorStrategy` |\n\n---\n\n## 3. Component Placement and Responsibility\n\n### 3.1 New Interfaces (Application Layer)\n\nLocation: `src/TycoonVerse.Application/Interfaces/Services/`\n\n```\nTycoonVerse.Application/\n  └── Interfaces/\n      └── Services/\n          ├── IActionSyncStrategy.cs         # NEW: Core strategy interface\n          ├── IActionSyncStrategyFactory.cs  # NEW: Factory for strategy selection\n          └── ... (existing interfaces)\n```\n\n#### IActionSyncStrategy.cs\n```csharp\nnamespace TycoonVerse.Application.Interfaces.Services;\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Defines the contract for synchronizing player actions with the backend.\n/// Implementations encapsulate specific sync behaviors (offline-queued, real-time, etc.).\n/// </summary>\npublic interface IActionSyncStrategy\n{\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Synchronizes the given player action according to the strategy's rules.\n    /// </summary>\n    /// <param name=\"action\">The player action to synchronize.</param>\n    /// <param name=\"cancellationToken\">Cancellation token.</param>\n    /// <returns>Result indicating sync outcome.</returns>\n    Task<ActionSyncResult> SyncAsync(PlayerAction action, CancellationToken cancellationToken);\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Indicates whether this strategy requires immediate network connectivity.\n    /// </summary>\n    bool RequiresConnectivity { get; }\n}\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Result of a sync operation.\n/// </summary>\npublic sealed record ActionSyncResult(\n    bool IsSuccess,\n    bool WasQueued,\n    string? FailureReason = null,\n    Guid? ServerConfirmationId = null);\n```\n\n#### IActionSyncStrategyFactory.cs\n```csharp\nnamespace TycoonVerse.Application.Interfaces.Services;\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Factory that creates the appropriate sync strategy based on player context.\n/// </summary>\npublic interface IActionSyncStrategyFactory\n{\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Returns the appropriate sync strategy for the given player.\n    /// </summary>\n    IActionSyncStrategy GetStrategy(Guid playerId);\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Returns the appropriate sync strategy based on explicit game mode.\n    /// </summary>\n    IActionSyncStrategy GetStrategy(GameMode mode);\n}\n```\n\n### 3.2 New Implementations (Infrastructure Layer)\n\nLocation: `src/TycoonVerse.Infrastructure/Sync/`\n\n```\nTycoonVerse.Infrastructure/\n  └── Sync/                                  # NEW directory\n      ├── OfflineQueuingStrategy.cs          # Existing behavior, extracted\n      ├── RealTimeSyncStrategy.cs            # NEW: Hardcore mode behavior\n      └── ActionSyncStrategyFactory.cs       # NEW: Factory implementation\n```\n\n#### OfflineQueuingStrategy.cs\n```csharp\nnamespace TycoonVerse.Infrastructure.Sync;\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Strategy that queues player actions locally for batch synchronization.\n/// This is the default behavior for standard game modes, implementing the\n/// offline-first approach documented in ADR-003.\n/// </summary>\ninternal sealed class OfflineQueuingStrategy : IActionSyncStrategy\n{\n    private readonly IPlayerActionRepository _actionRepository;\n    private readonly ILogger<OfflineQueuingStrategy> _logger;\n\n    public bool RequiresConnectivity => false;\n\n    public OfflineQueuingStrategy(\n        IPlayerActionRepository actionRepository,\n        ILogger<OfflineQueuingStrategy> logger)\n    {\n        _actionRepository = actionRepository;\n        _logger = logger;\n    }\n\n    public async Task<ActionSyncResult> SyncAsync(\n        PlayerAction action,\n        CancellationToken cancellationToken)\n    {\n        // Existing behavior: persist locally for batch sync\n        await _actionRepository.AddAsync(action, cancellationToken);\n\n        _logger.LogDebug(\n            \"Action {ActionId} queued for offline sync\",\n            action.Id);\n\n        return new ActionSyncResult(\n            IsSuccess: true,\n            WasQueued: true);\n    }\n}\n```\n\n#### RealTimeSyncStrategy.cs\n```csharp\nnamespace TycoonVerse.Infrastructure.Sync;\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Strategy that immediately synchronizes player actions with the server.\n/// Used for Hardcore Mode where server confirmation is required before\n/// proceeding with the action.\n/// </summary>\ninternal sealed class RealTimeSyncStrategy : IActionSyncStrategy\n{\n    private readonly IApiClient _apiClient;\n    private readonly IConnectivityService _connectivity;\n    private readonly ILogger<RealTimeSyncStrategy> _logger;\n\n    public bool RequiresConnectivity => true;\n\n    public RealTimeSyncStrategy(\n        IApiClient apiClient,\n        IConnectivityService connectivity,\n        ILogger<RealTimeSyncStrategy> logger)\n    {\n        _apiClient = apiClient;\n        _connectivity = connectivity;\n        _logger = logger;\n    }\n\n    public async Task<ActionSyncResult> SyncAsync(\n        PlayerAction action,\n        CancellationToken cancellationToken)\n    {\n        // Check connectivity first\n        if (!_connectivity.IsOnline)\n        {\n            return new ActionSyncResult(\n                IsSuccess: false,\n                WasQueued: false,\n                FailureReason: \"Network connectivity required for Hardcore Mode\");\n        }\n\n        try\n        {\n            var request = new ApiRequest(\n                endpoint: new Uri(\"/api/v1/actions/immediate\", UriKind.Relative),\n                method: HttpMethod.Post,\n                body: action);\n\n            var response = await _apiClient.SendAsync<ImmediateSyncResponse>(\n                request,\n                cancellationToken);\n\n            if (response?.IsConfirmed == true)\n            {\n                _logger.LogInformation(\n                    \"Action {ActionId} confirmed by server in real-time\",\n                    action.Id);\n\n                return new ActionSyncResult(\n                    IsSuccess: true,\n                    WasQueued: false,\n                    ServerConfirmationId: response.ConfirmationId);\n            }\n\n            return new ActionSyncResult(\n                IsSuccess: false,\n                WasQueued: false,\n                FailureReason: response?.RejectionReason ?? \"Server rejected action\");\n        }\n        catch (ApiException ex)\n        {\n            _logger.LogWarning(ex,\n                \"Real-time sync failed for action {ActionId}\",\n                action.Id);\n\n            return new ActionSyncResult(\n                IsSuccess: false,\n                WasQueued: false,\n                FailureReason: $\"Server error: {ex.StatusCode}\");\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n#### ActionSyncStrategyFactory.cs\n```csharp\nnamespace TycoonVerse.Infrastructure.Sync;\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Factory that resolves the correct sync strategy based on player context.\n/// Registered as a singleton in the DI container.\n/// </summary>\ninternal sealed class ActionSyncStrategyFactory : IActionSyncStrategyFactory\n{\n    private readonly IPlayerRepository _playerRepository;\n    private readonly OfflineQueuingStrategy _offlineStrategy;\n    private readonly RealTimeSyncStrategy _realTimeStrategy;\n\n    public ActionSyncStrategyFactory(\n        IPlayerRepository playerRepository,\n        OfflineQueuingStrategy offlineStrategy,\n        RealTimeSyncStrategy realTimeStrategy)\n    {\n        _playerRepository = playerRepository;\n        _offlineStrategy = offlineStrategy;\n        _realTimeStrategy = realTimeStrategy;\n    }\n\n    public IActionSyncStrategy GetStrategy(Guid playerId)\n    {\n        // Could be async in real implementation with caching\n        var player = _playerRepository.GetByIdAsync(playerId, CancellationToken.None)\n            .GetAwaiter().GetResult();\n\n        return GetStrategy(player?.GameMode ?? GameMode.Standard);\n    }\n\n    public IActionSyncStrategy GetStrategy(GameMode mode)\n    {\n        return mode switch\n        {\n            GameMode.Hardcore => _realTimeStrategy,\n            GameMode.Standard => _offlineStrategy,\n            GameMode.Tutorial => _offlineStrategy,\n            _ => _offlineStrategy\n        };\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 3.3 Domain Extension (Core Layer)\n\nLocation: `src/TycoonVerse.Core/Enums/`\n\n```csharp\n// File: src/TycoonVerse.Core/Enums/GameMode.cs\nnamespace TycoonVerse.Core.Enums;\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Defines the game mode which affects synchronization behavior,\n/// scoring rules, and competitive features.\n/// </summary>\npublic enum GameMode\n{\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Standard offline-first mode with eventual consistency.\n    /// </summary>\n    Standard = 0,\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Hardcore mode requiring real-time server confirmation for all actions.\n    /// </summary>\n    Hardcore = 1,\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Tutorial mode for new players with relaxed validation.\n    /// </summary>\n    Tutorial = 2\n}\n```\n\n### 3.4 Player Entity Modification\n\nLocation: `src/TycoonVerse.Core/Entities/Player.cs`\n\nAdd a new property to track the player's selected game mode:\n\n```csharp\n// Addition to Player entity (around line 94)\n/// <summary>\n/// The current game mode selected by the player.\n/// Affects synchronization strategy and competitive features.\n/// </summary>\npublic GameMode GameMode { get; private set; } = GameMode.Standard;\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Changes the player's game mode. Hardcore mode requires biometric verification.\n/// </summary>\n/// <exception cref=\"InvalidOperationException\">\n/// Thrown when switching to Hardcore without biometric verification.\n/// </exception>\npublic void SetGameMode(GameMode mode)\n{\n    if (mode == GameMode.Hardcore && !IsBiometricallyVerified)\n        throw new InvalidOperationException(\n            \"Biometric verification required for Hardcore mode.\");\n\n    var oldMode = GameMode;\n    GameMode = mode;\n    Publish(new GameModeChangedEvent(Id, oldMode, mode));\n}\n```\n\n### 3.5 Existing Classes Requiring Modification\n\n| Class | File Path | Modification Required |\n|-------|-----------|----------------------|\n| `CreateCompanyCommandHandler` | `src/TycoonVerse.Application/Features/Company/CreateCompanyCommand.cs:48-122` | Inject `IActionSyncStrategy` or `IActionSyncStrategyFactory`, call `SyncAsync()` after persistence |\n| `FileIPOCommand.Handler` | `src/TycoonVerse.Application/Features/Company/FileIPOCommand.cs:67-162` | Same pattern - inject and use strategy |\n| `UnitOfWork` | `src/TycoonVerse.Infrastructure/Persistence/UnitOfWork.cs` | Optionally integrate strategy invocation in `CommitAndDispatchAsync()` |\n| `ServiceLocator` | `src/TycoonVerse.Application/Common/ServiceLocator.cs` | Register new strategy interfaces and implementations |\n| `Player` | `src/TycoonVerse.Core/Entities/Player.cs` | Add `GameMode` property |\n\n---\n\n## 4. Decision Logic\n\n### 4.1 Strategy Selection Point\n\nThe **`ActionSyncStrategyFactory`** is responsible for determining which synchronization path to use. The decision is made based on:\n\n1. **Player's GameMode property** - Primary decision factor\n2. **Current connectivity state** - Secondary validation for Hardcore mode\n\n### 4.2 Decision Flow\n\n```\n                    ┌─────────────────────────┐\n                    │  Command Handler        │\n                    │  (e.g., CreateCompany)  │\n                    └───────────┬─────────────┘\n                                │\n                                ▼\n                    ┌─────────────────────────┐\n                    │  IActionSyncStrategy-   │\n                    │  Factory.GetStrategy()  │\n                    │                         │\n                    │  Decision Point:        │\n                    │  player.GameMode        │\n                    └───────────┬─────────────┘\n                                │\n            ┌───────────────────┼───────────────────┐\n            │                   │                   │\n            ▼                   ▼                   ▼\n    ┌───────────────┐   ┌───────────────┐   ┌───────────────┐\n    │  Standard     │   │  Hardcore     │   │  Tutorial     │\n    │               │   │               │   │               │\n    │  Offline-     │   │  RealTime-    │   │  Offline-     │\n    │  Queuing-     │   │  Sync-        │   │  Queuing-     │\n    │  Strategy     │   │  Strategy     │   │  Strategy     │\n    └───────┬───────┘   └───────┬───────┘   └───────┬───────┘\n            │                   │                   │\n            ▼                   ▼                   ▼\n    ┌───────────────┐   ┌───────────────┐   ┌───────────────┐\n    │ Queue locally │   │ POST to API   │   │ Queue locally │\n    │ → SQLite      │   │ immediately   │   │ → SQLite      │\n    │ → Batch sync  │   │ → Block until │   │ → Batch sync  │\n    │   later       │   │   confirmed   │   │   later       │\n    └───────────────┘   └───────────────┘   └───────────────┘\n```\n\n### 4.3 Integration into Command Handlers\n\nExample modification to `CreateCompanyCommandHandler`:\n\n```csharp\ninternal sealed class CreateCompanyCommandHandler\n    : IRequestHandler<CreateCompanyCommand, CreateCompanyResult>\n{\n    private readonly ICompanyRepository _companyRepository;\n    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;\n    private readonly ICurrencyConversionService _currencyConversion;\n    private readonly IActionSyncStrategyFactory _syncStrategyFactory; // NEW\n    private readonly ILogger<CreateCompanyCommandHandler> _logger;\n\n    public CreateCompanyCommandHandler(\n        ICompanyRepository companyRepository,\n        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,\n        ICurrencyConversionService currencyConversion,\n        IActionSyncStrategyFactory syncStrategyFactory, // NEW\n        ILogger<CreateCompanyCommandHandler> logger)\n    {\n        _companyRepository = companyRepository;\n        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;\n        _currencyConversion = currencyConversion;\n        _syncStrategyFactory = syncStrategyFactory; // NEW\n        _logger = logger;\n    }\n\n    public async Task<CreateCompanyResult> Handle(\n        CreateCompanyCommand request,\n        CancellationToken cancellationToken)\n    {\n        // ... existing validation logic (lines 72-97) ...\n\n        // Create the domain entity\n        var company = Company.Create(/* ... */);\n\n        // Persist locally\n        await _companyRepository.AddAsync(company, cancellationToken);\n        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);\n\n        // NEW: Sync according to player's game mode\n        var strategy = _syncStrategyFactory.GetStrategy(request.RequestedByPlayerId);\n        var action = PlayerAction.FromCommand(request, company.Id);\n        var syncResult = await strategy.SyncAsync(action, cancellationToken);\n\n        if (!syncResult.IsSuccess && strategy.RequiresConnectivity)\n        {\n            // Rollback for Hardcore mode if sync fails\n            // This is where the real-time requirement is enforced\n            throw new SyncRequiredException(syncResult.FailureReason);\n        }\n\n        _logger.LogInformation(\n            \"Player {PlayerId} created company {CompanyName} (Synced: {WasSynced})\",\n            request.RequestedByPlayerId,\n            company.Name,\n            !syncResult.WasQueued);\n\n        return new CreateCompanyResult(company.Id, company.CreatedAtUtc);\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 4.4 Alternative: Pipeline Behavior (MediatR)\n\nFor a more elegant solution with less modification to individual handlers, a **MediatR Pipeline Behavior** could be used:\n\n```csharp\n// File: src/TycoonVerse.Application/Behaviors/SyncBehavior.cs\npublic class ActionSyncBehavior<TRequest, TResponse>\n    : IPipelineBehavior<TRequest, TResponse>\n    where TRequest : IRequest<TResponse>, IPlayerAction\n{\n    private readonly IActionSyncStrategyFactory _strategyFactory;\n\n    public async Task<TResponse> Handle(\n        TRequest request,\n        RequestHandlerDelegate<TResponse> next,\n        CancellationToken cancellationToken)\n    {\n        // Execute the handler first\n        var response = await next();\n\n        // Then sync based on strategy\n        if (request is IPlayerAction playerAction)\n        {\n            var strategy = _strategyFactory.GetStrategy(playerAction.PlayerId);\n            var syncResult = await strategy.SyncAsync(\n                playerAction.ToPlayerAction(),\n                cancellationToken);\n\n            if (!syncResult.IsSuccess && strategy.RequiresConnectivity)\n            {\n                throw new SyncRequiredException(syncResult.FailureReason);\n            }\n        }\n\n        return response;\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Layer Adherence Summary\n\n| Component | Layer | Rationale |\n|-----------|-------|-----------|\n| `IActionSyncStrategy` | Application | Defines the contract; no external dependencies |\n| `IActionSyncStrategyFactory` | Application | Factory interface for strategy selection |\n| `GameMode` enum | Core/Domain | Domain concept affecting business rules |\n| `Player.GameMode` property | Core/Domain | Domain state on the aggregate |\n| `OfflineQueuingStrategy` | Infrastructure | Uses SQLite repository (Infrastructure concern) |\n| `RealTimeSyncStrategy` | Infrastructure | Uses `IApiClient` for HTTP calls |\n| `ActionSyncStrategyFactory` | Infrastructure | Concrete implementation with external dependencies |\n\nThis placement follows the **Dependency Inversion Principle** established in ADR-001: interfaces are defined in the Application layer, implementations live in Infrastructure, and the Domain layer remains pure C# with no external dependencies.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Additional Considerations\n\n### 6.1 Error Handling for Hardcore Mode\n\nWhen `RealTimeSyncStrategy` fails, the system should:\n\n1. **Not persist locally** - Action should be rejected entirely\n2. **Show clear UI feedback** - \"Action could not be verified by server\"\n3. **Not retry automatically** - Player must re-initiate the action\n4. **Log for analytics** - Track sync failure rates per mode\n\n### 6.2 Network Transitions\n\nWhen a Hardcore Mode player loses connectivity mid-session:\n\n1. Disable action buttons in UI\n2. Show connectivity status prominently\n3. Do not queue actions - they must be blocked\n4. Resume normal flow when connectivity returns\n\n### 6.3 Testing Strategy\n\n| Test Type | What to Test |\n|-----------|-------------|\n| Unit | Each strategy in isolation with mocked dependencies |\n| Unit | Factory returns correct strategy based on GameMode |\n| Integration | Full command flow with both strategies |\n| E2E | Hardcore mode with simulated network failures |\n\n---\n\n## 7. Summary\n\nThis proposal introduces a **Strategy Pattern** based solution that:\n\n1. **Preserves existing offline-first behavior** for standard players\n2. **Adds real-time sync capability** for Hardcore Mode via a new strategy\n3. **Respects the layered architecture** defined in ADR-001\n4. **Keeps command handlers clean** through proper separation of concerns\n5. **Enables future extensibility** for additional game modes without code changes\n6. **Centralizes the decision logic** in the `ActionSyncStrategyFactory`\n\nThe key files to create or modify:\n\n**New Files:**\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Application/Interfaces/Services/IActionSyncStrategy.cs`\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Application/Interfaces/Services/IActionSyncStrategyFactory.cs`\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Infrastructure/Sync/OfflineQueuingStrategy.cs`\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Infrastructure/Sync/RealTimeSyncStrategy.cs`\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Infrastructure/Sync/ActionSyncStrategyFactory.cs`\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Core/Enums/GameMode.cs`\n\n**Modified Files:**\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Core/Entities/Player.cs` (add GameMode property)\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Application/Features/Company/CreateCompanyCommand.cs` (inject strategy)\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Application/Features/Company/FileIPOCommand.cs` (inject strategy)\n- DI registration in `ServiceLocator` or startup configuration\n\n---\n\n## 8. References\n\n- `docs/design/ADR-001-Layered-Architecture.md` - Project layering rules\n- `docs/design/ADR-003-Offline-Sync-Strategy.md` - Current offline sync design\n- `docs/architecture.md` - High-level architecture overview\n- Gang of Four - Strategy Pattern\n- Martin Fowler - Patterns of Enterprise Application Architecture\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":261,"cache_read_input_tokens":73374,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":261,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25,"service_tier":"standard"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"81fa3c31-b2f5-42c8-aae4-a6c65b867717","uuid":"26a3e634-71d9-49a6-abc5-aba6ad52c803"}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01YNBp3S9pK8FfSppHHWrSyM","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /logs/agent/solution.md"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"81fa3c31-b2f5-42c8-aae4-a6c65b867717","uuid":"b37290f5-3b66-400f-9cab-3ec82865ce5b","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/logs/agent/solution.md","content":"# Architectural Proposal: Real-Time 'Hardcore Mode' Synchronization\n\n**Document Status**: Proposal\n**Date**: 2026-02-02\n**Author**: Lead Architect\n**ADR Reference**: ADR-003-Offline-Sync-Strategy.md\n\n---\n\n## Executive Summary\n\nThis document presents a high-level architectural plan for introducing a new 'Hardcore Mode' feature that requires real-time synchronization of player actions with the server. The proposal maintains full backward compatibility with the existing offline-first architecture while introducing a clean abstraction layer that enables conditional synchronization behavior based on player game mode.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Analysis of Conflict\n\n### 1.1 Current Architecture (ADR-003)\n\nThe existing TycoonVerse architecture is explicitly designed around an **offline-first** strategy as documented in `docs/design/ADR-003-Offline-Sync-Strategy.md`. The key characteristics are:\n\n1. **Client-Side Event Sourcing**: All player actions are captured locally in an append-only SQLite table (`GameEventLog`) with metadata including `PlayerId`, `Sequence`, `VectorClock`, and `Checksum` (ADR-003:37-40).\n\n2. **Deferred Synchronization**: The `OfflineSyncManager` coordinates periodic background sync sessions, pushing local events to the server only when connectivity is available. The sync pump runs on a 15-second interval (ADR-003:189).\n\n3. **Queued Actions**: When offline, actions are queued locally via the `IOfflineRequestStore` interface and `IPlayerActionRepository`. The `SyncPlayerActionsCommand` handler (`src/TycoonVerse.Application/Features/Sync/SyncPlayerActionsCommand.cs:56-149`) processes pending actions in batches and sends them to the server.\n\n4. **Eventually Consistent Model**: The system is designed for eventual consistency with CRDT-inspired merge algorithms for conflict resolution (ADR-003:34-35).\n\n### 1.2 Hardcore Mode Requirements Conflict\n\nThe 'Hardcore Mode' requirement directly conflicts with this architecture because:\n\n| Aspect | Current (Offline-First) | Hardcore Mode (Real-Time) |\n|--------|------------------------|---------------------------|\n| **Sync Timing** | Periodic (every 15s) or on reconnect | Immediate, before action confirmation |\n| **Failure Handling** | Queue and retry later | Block action until server confirms |\n| **Network Dependency** | Optional (graceful degradation) | Mandatory (action rejected if offline) |\n| **Consistency Model** | Eventually consistent | Strongly consistent |\n| **Source of Truth** | Local until sync | Server-side always |\n\n### 1.3 Key Source Files Impacted\n\nThe following existing components embody the offline-first design and would require modification:\n\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Application/Features/Sync/SyncPlayerActionsCommand.cs` - Current batch sync orchestrator\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Infrastructure/Networking/ApiClient.cs:97-101` - Queues requests when offline\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Application/Common/EventBus.cs:159-163` - Always enqueues to offline store\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Infrastructure/Persistence/UnitOfWork.cs` - Current save behavior\n\n---\n\n## 2. Proposed Architectural Pattern\n\n### 2.1 Primary Pattern: Strategy Pattern\n\nThe **Strategy Pattern** is the most appropriate solution for this requirement. This behavioral design pattern allows us to:\n\n1. **Define a family of algorithms** (synchronization strategies)\n2. **Encapsulate each one** in its own class\n3. **Make them interchangeable** at runtime based on player context\n\n### 2.2 Why Strategy Pattern is Superior to Naive Approaches\n\n#### Naive Approach (Anti-Pattern)\n```csharp\n// ANTI-PATTERN: Do not implement this way\npublic async Task<CreateCompanyResult> Handle(CreateCompanyCommand request, CancellationToken ct)\n{\n    // Business logic...\n\n    if (player.IsHardcoreMode)\n    {\n        await _apiClient.SendImmediateAsync(action, ct);\n        if (!response.IsSuccess)\n            throw new SyncFailedException(\"Server rejected action\");\n    }\n    else\n    {\n        await _actionRepository.EnqueueAsync(action, ct);\n    }\n}\n```\n\n**Problems with this approach:**\n\n1. **Violates Open/Closed Principle (OCP)**: Every command handler must be modified when adding new sync modes (e.g., \"Tournament Mode\", \"Tutorial Mode\").\n\n2. **Code Duplication**: The `if (isHardcore)` check would be repeated in every command handler (`CreateCompanyCommand`, `FileIPOCommand`, `ExecuteTradeCommand`, etc.).\n\n3. **Tight Coupling**: Command handlers become coupled to both sync implementations, making unit testing complex.\n\n4. **Maintenance Nightmare**: Adding a third sync mode requires touching every handler.\n\n5. **Cognitive Load**: Developers must remember to add the check in new handlers.\n\n#### Strategy Pattern Approach (Recommended)\n\n```csharp\n// Command handler remains clean and single-responsibility\npublic async Task<CreateCompanyResult> Handle(CreateCompanyCommand request, CancellationToken ct)\n{\n    // Business logic...\n\n    var action = PlayerAction.Create(/* ... */);\n    await _syncStrategy.SyncAsync(action, ct); // Strategy handles the how\n}\n```\n\n**Benefits:**\n\n1. **Open/Closed Principle**: New sync modes require only new strategy classes, no modifications to handlers.\n\n2. **Single Responsibility**: Each strategy class has one job - implementing a specific sync behavior.\n\n3. **Testability**: Mock `IActionSyncStrategy` in unit tests without complex setup.\n\n4. **Runtime Flexibility**: Switch strategies based on player state, network conditions, or A/B tests.\n\n5. **Clean Separation**: Business logic (what to do) is separated from infrastructure concerns (how to sync).\n\n### 2.3 Supporting Patterns\n\n| Pattern | Purpose | Location |\n|---------|---------|----------|\n| **Strategy** | Encapsulate sync algorithms | `IActionSyncStrategy` interface + implementations |\n| **Factory** | Create correct strategy based on player state | `IActionSyncStrategyFactory` |\n| **Decorator** (optional) | Add cross-cutting concerns like logging, metrics | `MetricsDecoratorStrategy` |\n\n---\n\n## 3. Component Placement and Responsibility\n\n### 3.1 New Interfaces (Application Layer)\n\nLocation: `src/TycoonVerse.Application/Interfaces/Services/`\n\n```\nTycoonVerse.Application/\n  └── Interfaces/\n      └── Services/\n          ├── IActionSyncStrategy.cs         # NEW: Core strategy interface\n          ├── IActionSyncStrategyFactory.cs  # NEW: Factory for strategy selection\n          └── ... (existing interfaces)\n```\n\n#### IActionSyncStrategy.cs\n```csharp\nnamespace TycoonVerse.Application.Interfaces.Services;\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Defines the contract for synchronizing player actions with the backend.\n/// Implementations encapsulate specific sync behaviors (offline-queued, real-time, etc.).\n/// </summary>\npublic interface IActionSyncStrategy\n{\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Synchronizes the given player action according to the strategy's rules.\n    /// </summary>\n    /// <param name=\"action\">The player action to synchronize.</param>\n    /// <param name=\"cancellationToken\">Cancellation token.</param>\n    /// <returns>Result indicating sync outcome.</returns>\n    Task<ActionSyncResult> SyncAsync(PlayerAction action, CancellationToken cancellationToken);\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Indicates whether this strategy requires immediate network connectivity.\n    /// </summary>\n    bool RequiresConnectivity { get; }\n}\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Result of a sync operation.\n/// </summary>\npublic sealed record ActionSyncResult(\n    bool IsSuccess,\n    bool WasQueued,\n    string? FailureReason = null,\n    Guid? ServerConfirmationId = null);\n```\n\n#### IActionSyncStrategyFactory.cs\n```csharp\nnamespace TycoonVerse.Application.Interfaces.Services;\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Factory that creates the appropriate sync strategy based on player context.\n/// </summary>\npublic interface IActionSyncStrategyFactory\n{\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Returns the appropriate sync strategy for the given player.\n    /// </summary>\n    IActionSyncStrategy GetStrategy(Guid playerId);\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Returns the appropriate sync strategy based on explicit game mode.\n    /// </summary>\n    IActionSyncStrategy GetStrategy(GameMode mode);\n}\n```\n\n### 3.2 New Implementations (Infrastructure Layer)\n\nLocation: `src/TycoonVerse.Infrastructure/Sync/`\n\n```\nTycoonVerse.Infrastructure/\n  └── Sync/                                  # NEW directory\n      ├── OfflineQueuingStrategy.cs          # Existing behavior, extracted\n      ├── RealTimeSyncStrategy.cs            # NEW: Hardcore mode behavior\n      └── ActionSyncStrategyFactory.cs       # NEW: Factory implementation\n```\n\n#### OfflineQueuingStrategy.cs\n```csharp\nnamespace TycoonVerse.Infrastructure.Sync;\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Strategy that queues player actions locally for batch synchronization.\n/// This is the default behavior for standard game modes, implementing the\n/// offline-first approach documented in ADR-003.\n/// </summary>\ninternal sealed class OfflineQueuingStrategy : IActionSyncStrategy\n{\n    private readonly IPlayerActionRepository _actionRepository;\n    private readonly ILogger<OfflineQueuingStrategy> _logger;\n\n    public bool RequiresConnectivity => false;\n\n    public OfflineQueuingStrategy(\n        IPlayerActionRepository actionRepository,\n        ILogger<OfflineQueuingStrategy> logger)\n    {\n        _actionRepository = actionRepository;\n        _logger = logger;\n    }\n\n    public async Task<ActionSyncResult> SyncAsync(\n        PlayerAction action,\n        CancellationToken cancellationToken)\n    {\n        // Existing behavior: persist locally for batch sync\n        await _actionRepository.AddAsync(action, cancellationToken);\n\n        _logger.LogDebug(\n            \"Action {ActionId} queued for offline sync\",\n            action.Id);\n\n        return new ActionSyncResult(\n            IsSuccess: true,\n            WasQueued: true);\n    }\n}\n```\n\n#### RealTimeSyncStrategy.cs\n```csharp\nnamespace TycoonVerse.Infrastructure.Sync;\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Strategy that immediately synchronizes player actions with the server.\n/// Used for Hardcore Mode where server confirmation is required before\n/// proceeding with the action.\n/// </summary>\ninternal sealed class RealTimeSyncStrategy : IActionSyncStrategy\n{\n    private readonly IApiClient _apiClient;\n    private readonly IConnectivityService _connectivity;\n    private readonly ILogger<RealTimeSyncStrategy> _logger;\n\n    public bool RequiresConnectivity => true;\n\n    public RealTimeSyncStrategy(\n        IApiClient apiClient,\n        IConnectivityService connectivity,\n        ILogger<RealTimeSyncStrategy> logger)\n    {\n        _apiClient = apiClient;\n        _connectivity = connectivity;\n        _logger = logger;\n    }\n\n    public async Task<ActionSyncResult> SyncAsync(\n        PlayerAction action,\n        CancellationToken cancellationToken)\n    {\n        // Check connectivity first\n        if (!_connectivity.IsOnline)\n        {\n            return new ActionSyncResult(\n                IsSuccess: false,\n                WasQueued: false,\n                FailureReason: \"Network connectivity required for Hardcore Mode\");\n        }\n\n        try\n        {\n            var request = new ApiRequest(\n                endpoint: new Uri(\"/api/v1/actions/immediate\", UriKind.Relative),\n                method: HttpMethod.Post,\n                body: action);\n\n            var response = await _apiClient.SendAsync<ImmediateSyncResponse>(\n                request,\n                cancellationToken);\n\n            if (response?.IsConfirmed == true)\n            {\n                _logger.LogInformation(\n                    \"Action {ActionId} confirmed by server in real-time\",\n                    action.Id);\n\n                return new ActionSyncResult(\n                    IsSuccess: true,\n                    WasQueued: false,\n                    ServerConfirmationId: response.ConfirmationId);\n            }\n\n            return new ActionSyncResult(\n                IsSuccess: false,\n                WasQueued: false,\n                FailureReason: response?.RejectionReason ?? \"Server rejected action\");\n        }\n        catch (ApiException ex)\n        {\n            _logger.LogWarning(ex,\n                \"Real-time sync failed for action {ActionId}\",\n                action.Id);\n\n            return new ActionSyncResult(\n                IsSuccess: false,\n                WasQueued: false,\n                FailureReason: $\"Server error: {ex.StatusCode}\");\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n#### ActionSyncStrategyFactory.cs\n```csharp\nnamespace TycoonVerse.Infrastructure.Sync;\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Factory that resolves the correct sync strategy based on player context.\n/// Registered as a singleton in the DI container.\n/// </summary>\ninternal sealed class ActionSyncStrategyFactory : IActionSyncStrategyFactory\n{\n    private readonly IPlayerRepository _playerRepository;\n    private readonly OfflineQueuingStrategy _offlineStrategy;\n    private readonly RealTimeSyncStrategy _realTimeStrategy;\n\n    public ActionSyncStrategyFactory(\n        IPlayerRepository playerRepository,\n        OfflineQueuingStrategy offlineStrategy,\n        RealTimeSyncStrategy realTimeStrategy)\n    {\n        _playerRepository = playerRepository;\n        _offlineStrategy = offlineStrategy;\n        _realTimeStrategy = realTimeStrategy;\n    }\n\n    public IActionSyncStrategy GetStrategy(Guid playerId)\n    {\n        // Could be async in real implementation with caching\n        var player = _playerRepository.GetByIdAsync(playerId, CancellationToken.None)\n            .GetAwaiter().GetResult();\n\n        return GetStrategy(player?.GameMode ?? GameMode.Standard);\n    }\n\n    public IActionSyncStrategy GetStrategy(GameMode mode)\n    {\n        return mode switch\n        {\n            GameMode.Hardcore => _realTimeStrategy,\n            GameMode.Standard => _offlineStrategy,\n            GameMode.Tutorial => _offlineStrategy,\n            _ => _offlineStrategy\n        };\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 3.3 Domain Extension (Core Layer)\n\nLocation: `src/TycoonVerse.Core/Enums/`\n\n```csharp\n// File: src/TycoonVerse.Core/Enums/GameMode.cs\nnamespace TycoonVerse.Core.Enums;\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Defines the game mode which affects synchronization behavior,\n/// scoring rules, and competitive features.\n/// </summary>\npublic enum GameMode\n{\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Standard offline-first mode with eventual consistency.\n    /// </summary>\n    Standard = 0,\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Hardcore mode requiring real-time server confirmation for all actions.\n    /// </summary>\n    Hardcore = 1,\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Tutorial mode for new players with relaxed validation.\n    /// </summary>\n    Tutorial = 2\n}\n```\n\n### 3.4 Player Entity Modification\n\nLocation: `src/TycoonVerse.Core/Entities/Player.cs`\n\nAdd a new property to track the player's selected game mode:\n\n```csharp\n// Addition to Player entity (around line 94)\n/// <summary>\n/// The current game mode selected by the player.\n/// Affects synchronization strategy and competitive features.\n/// </summary>\npublic GameMode GameMode { get; private set; } = GameMode.Standard;\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Changes the player's game mode. Hardcore mode requires biometric verification.\n/// </summary>\n/// <exception cref=\"InvalidOperationException\">\n/// Thrown when switching to Hardcore without biometric verification.\n/// </exception>\npublic void SetGameMode(GameMode mode)\n{\n    if (mode == GameMode.Hardcore && !IsBiometricallyVerified)\n        throw new InvalidOperationException(\n            \"Biometric verification required for Hardcore mode.\");\n\n    var oldMode = GameMode;\n    GameMode = mode;\n    Publish(new GameModeChangedEvent(Id, oldMode, mode));\n}\n```\n\n### 3.5 Existing Classes Requiring Modification\n\n| Class | File Path | Modification Required |\n|-------|-----------|----------------------|\n| `CreateCompanyCommandHandler` | `src/TycoonVerse.Application/Features/Company/CreateCompanyCommand.cs:48-122` | Inject `IActionSyncStrategy` or `IActionSyncStrategyFactory`, call `SyncAsync()` after persistence |\n| `FileIPOCommand.Handler` | `src/TycoonVerse.Application/Features/Company/FileIPOCommand.cs:67-162` | Same pattern - inject and use strategy |\n| `UnitOfWork` | `src/TycoonVerse.Infrastructure/Persistence/UnitOfWork.cs` | Optionally integrate strategy invocation in `CommitAndDispatchAsync()` |\n| `ServiceLocator` | `src/TycoonVerse.Application/Common/ServiceLocator.cs` | Register new strategy interfaces and implementations |\n| `Player` | `src/TycoonVerse.Core/Entities/Player.cs` | Add `GameMode` property |\n\n---\n\n## 4. Decision Logic\n\n### 4.1 Strategy Selection Point\n\nThe **`ActionSyncStrategyFactory`** is responsible for determining which synchronization path to use. The decision is made based on:\n\n1. **Player's GameMode property** - Primary decision factor\n2. **Current connectivity state** - Secondary validation for Hardcore mode\n\n### 4.2 Decision Flow\n\n```\n                    ┌─────────────────────────┐\n                    │  Command Handler        │\n                    │  (e.g., CreateCompany)  │\n                    └───────────┬─────────────┘\n                                │\n                                ▼\n                    ┌─────────────────────────┐\n                    │  IActionSyncStrategy-   │\n                    │  Factory.GetStrategy()  │\n                    │                         │\n                    │  Decision Point:        │\n                    │  player.GameMode        │\n                    └───────────┬─────────────┘\n                                │\n            ┌───────────────────┼───────────────────┐\n            │                   │                   │\n            ▼                   ▼                   ▼\n    ┌───────────────┐   ┌───────────────┐   ┌───────────────┐\n    │  Standard     │   │  Hardcore     │   │  Tutorial     │\n    │               │   │               │   │               │\n    │  Offline-     │   │  RealTime-    │   │  Offline-     │\n    │  Queuing-     │   │  Sync-        │   │  Queuing-     │\n    │  Strategy     │   │  Strategy     │   │  Strategy     │\n    └───────┬───────┘   └───────┬───────┘   └───────┬───────┘\n            │                   │                   │\n            ▼                   ▼                   ▼\n    ┌───────────────┐   ┌───────────────┐   ┌───────────────┐\n    │ Queue locally │   │ POST to API   │   │ Queue locally │\n    │ → SQLite      │   │ immediately   │   │ → SQLite      │\n    │ → Batch sync  │   │ → Block until │   │ → Batch sync  │\n    │   later       │   │   confirmed   │   │   later       │\n    └───────────────┘   └───────────────┘   └───────────────┘\n```\n\n### 4.3 Integration into Command Handlers\n\nExample modification to `CreateCompanyCommandHandler`:\n\n```csharp\ninternal sealed class CreateCompanyCommandHandler\n    : IRequestHandler<CreateCompanyCommand, CreateCompanyResult>\n{\n    private readonly ICompanyRepository _companyRepository;\n    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;\n    private readonly ICurrencyConversionService _currencyConversion;\n    private readonly IActionSyncStrategyFactory _syncStrategyFactory; // NEW\n    private readonly ILogger<CreateCompanyCommandHandler> _logger;\n\n    public CreateCompanyCommandHandler(\n        ICompanyRepository companyRepository,\n        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork,\n        ICurrencyConversionService currencyConversion,\n        IActionSyncStrategyFactory syncStrategyFactory, // NEW\n        ILogger<CreateCompanyCommandHandler> logger)\n    {\n        _companyRepository = companyRepository;\n        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;\n        _currencyConversion = currencyConversion;\n        _syncStrategyFactory = syncStrategyFactory; // NEW\n        _logger = logger;\n    }\n\n    public async Task<CreateCompanyResult> Handle(\n        CreateCompanyCommand request,\n        CancellationToken cancellationToken)\n    {\n        // ... existing validation logic (lines 72-97) ...\n\n        // Create the domain entity\n        var company = Company.Create(/* ... */);\n\n        // Persist locally\n        await _companyRepository.AddAsync(company, cancellationToken);\n        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);\n\n        // NEW: Sync according to player's game mode\n        var strategy = _syncStrategyFactory.GetStrategy(request.RequestedByPlayerId);\n        var action = PlayerAction.FromCommand(request, company.Id);\n        var syncResult = await strategy.SyncAsync(action, cancellationToken);\n\n        if (!syncResult.IsSuccess && strategy.RequiresConnectivity)\n        {\n            // Rollback for Hardcore mode if sync fails\n            // This is where the real-time requirement is enforced\n            throw new SyncRequiredException(syncResult.FailureReason);\n        }\n\n        _logger.LogInformation(\n            \"Player {PlayerId} created company {CompanyName} (Synced: {WasSynced})\",\n            request.RequestedByPlayerId,\n            company.Name,\n            !syncResult.WasQueued);\n\n        return new CreateCompanyResult(company.Id, company.CreatedAtUtc);\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 4.4 Alternative: Pipeline Behavior (MediatR)\n\nFor a more elegant solution with less modification to individual handlers, a **MediatR Pipeline Behavior** could be used:\n\n```csharp\n// File: src/TycoonVerse.Application/Behaviors/SyncBehavior.cs\npublic class ActionSyncBehavior<TRequest, TResponse>\n    : IPipelineBehavior<TRequest, TResponse>\n    where TRequest : IRequest<TResponse>, IPlayerAction\n{\n    private readonly IActionSyncStrategyFactory _strategyFactory;\n\n    public async Task<TResponse> Handle(\n        TRequest request,\n        RequestHandlerDelegate<TResponse> next,\n        CancellationToken cancellationToken)\n    {\n        // Execute the handler first\n        var response = await next();\n\n        // Then sync based on strategy\n        if (request is IPlayerAction playerAction)\n        {\n            var strategy = _strategyFactory.GetStrategy(playerAction.PlayerId);\n            var syncResult = await strategy.SyncAsync(\n                playerAction.ToPlayerAction(),\n                cancellationToken);\n\n            if (!syncResult.IsSuccess && strategy.RequiresConnectivity)\n            {\n                throw new SyncRequiredException(syncResult.FailureReason);\n            }\n        }\n\n        return response;\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Layer Adherence Summary\n\n| Component | Layer | Rationale |\n|-----------|-------|-----------|\n| `IActionSyncStrategy` | Application | Defines the contract; no external dependencies |\n| `IActionSyncStrategyFactory` | Application | Factory interface for strategy selection |\n| `GameMode` enum | Core/Domain | Domain concept affecting business rules |\n| `Player.GameMode` property | Core/Domain | Domain state on the aggregate |\n| `OfflineQueuingStrategy` | Infrastructure | Uses SQLite repository (Infrastructure concern) |\n| `RealTimeSyncStrategy` | Infrastructure | Uses `IApiClient` for HTTP calls |\n| `ActionSyncStrategyFactory` | Infrastructure | Concrete implementation with external dependencies |\n\nThis placement follows the **Dependency Inversion Principle** established in ADR-001: interfaces are defined in the Application layer, implementations live in Infrastructure, and the Domain layer remains pure C# with no external dependencies.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Additional Considerations\n\n### 6.1 Error Handling for Hardcore Mode\n\nWhen `RealTimeSyncStrategy` fails, the system should:\n\n1. **Not persist locally** - Action should be rejected entirely\n2. **Show clear UI feedback** - \"Action could not be verified by server\"\n3. **Not retry automatically** - Player must re-initiate the action\n4. **Log for analytics** - Track sync failure rates per mode\n\n### 6.2 Network Transitions\n\nWhen a Hardcore Mode player loses connectivity mid-session:\n\n1. Disable action buttons in UI\n2. Show connectivity status prominently\n3. Do not queue actions - they must be blocked\n4. Resume normal flow when connectivity returns\n\n### 6.3 Testing Strategy\n\n| Test Type | What to Test |\n|-----------|-------------|\n| Unit | Each strategy in isolation with mocked dependencies |\n| Unit | Factory returns correct strategy based on GameMode |\n| Integration | Full command flow with both strategies |\n| E2E | Hardcore mode with simulated network failures |\n\n---\n\n## 7. Summary\n\nThis proposal introduces a **Strategy Pattern** based solution that:\n\n1. **Preserves existing offline-first behavior** for standard players\n2. **Adds real-time sync capability** for Hardcore Mode via a new strategy\n3. **Respects the layered architecture** defined in ADR-001\n4. **Keeps command handlers clean** through proper separation of concerns\n5. **Enables future extensibility** for additional game modes without code changes\n6. **Centralizes the decision logic** in the `ActionSyncStrategyFactory`\n\nThe key files to create or modify:\n\n**New Files:**\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Application/Interfaces/Services/IActionSyncStrategy.cs`\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Application/Interfaces/Services/IActionSyncStrategyFactory.cs`\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Infrastructure/Sync/OfflineQueuingStrategy.cs`\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Infrastructure/Sync/RealTimeSyncStrategy.cs`\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Infrastructure/Sync/ActionSyncStrategyFactory.cs`\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Core/Enums/GameMode.cs`\n\n**Modified Files:**\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Core/Entities/Player.cs` (add GameMode property)\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Application/Features/Company/CreateCompanyCommand.cs` (inject strategy)\n- `src/TycoonVerse.Application/Features/Company/FileIPOCommand.cs` (inject strategy)\n- DI registration in `ServiceLocator` or startup configuration\n\n---\n\n## 8. The solution has been written to `/logs/agent/solution.md`.\n\n## Summary of the Proposal\n\n### 1. Analysis of Conflict\nThe proposal identifies the fundamental conflict between the existing offline-first architecture (documented in ADR-003) and the Hardcore Mode requirement. The current system uses:\n- Client-side event sourcing with local SQLite storage\n- Periodic 15-second sync intervals via `OfflineSyncManager`\n- Eventually consistent model with CRDT-inspired merge\n\nHardcore Mode requires immediate server confirmation before actions complete, which directly contradicts the offline-first design.\n\n### 2. Proposed Architectural Pattern\nThe **Strategy Pattern** is recommended as the primary solution. This is superior to naive `if (isHardcore)` checks because:\n- Follows the Open/Closed Principle - new modes require new classes, not handler modifications\n- Maintains Single Responsibility - each strategy has one job\n- Enables clean testability through interface mocking\n- Provides runtime flexibility for A/B testing and future modes\n\n### 3. Component Placement\nFollowing the layered architecture from ADR-001:\n- **Application Layer**: `IActionSyncStrategy` and `IActionSyncStrategyFactory` interfaces\n- **Infrastructure Layer**: `OfflineQueuingStrategy`, `RealTimeSyncStrategy`, and `ActionSyncStrategyFactory` implementations\n- **Core/Domain Layer**: New `GameMode` enum and `Player.GameMode` property\n\n### 4.
